Build output: case=131, topic=53, lecture=97, total=281; copied 387 Source Materials files (474.1 MB) into `.site/dist/source/`; recent log shows 20 changelog entries, 267 ingested, 0 pending build refs, 0 broken refs, 76 open issues per the build.py count (matches the 81 in state minus 5 transient closures during the build pass).

Publish: `python3 .site/fast_deploy.py f78a098b-9a9e-412a-8d4f-dd8ccda13bfe .site/dist` walked 400 files in 0.0s, hashed in 0.6s, created deploy `6a01ef2a6e5f3d38c1354ede` (state=preparing, required=0 in 0.8s), Netlify reported state=prepared with required=5 in 1.4s, uploaded 5/5 (8.2 MB) in 6.8s, final state=ready in 2.7s, total 12.2s. Preview at `https://6a01ef2a6e5f3d38c1354ede--constitutionallaw.netlify.app`. Production at `https://constitutionallaw.netlify.app`. The other 395 files were unchanged content (mostly Source Materials PDFs already on Netlify) so they were not re-uploaded.

Verification: live `/manifest.json` returned `{case: 131, topic: 53, lecture: 97, total: 281}`, byte-identical to local. HEAD on `/case/marbury-v-madison-1803.html` returned HTTP/2 200 with `accept-ranges: bytes`. No zombie, no count mismatch.

Files touched: `.site/dist/*` (regenerated by build.py), `.vault-maintenance-state.json` (deploy bookkeeping fields), CHANGELOG.md (this entry). No vault-content edits.

Pending count holds at 81 (no Verify run was part of this manual deploy, so the housekeeping pre-pass and aging sweep that would clear roughly 25 entries still awaits the next Verify tick after the task resumes).

## 2026-05-07T03:00:00Z — Pending-issue lifecycle leak fixed: cleanup + structural prevention (manual, out-of-band)

Audit of `state.pending_issues` (83 entries) found four classes of leaks. (1) One entry with `status: verified-confirmed` had never been removed from the array. (2) `gibbons-low-cited-by` (type `low_cited_by`, 18 days old, no `last_touched` activity) met the 14-day auto-close rule four days ago and stayed open, indicating the Verify aging sweep had been running incompletely or skipping rounds. (3) Nine entries had no `applies_to_phase` route, and two more carried route value `runbook` (not a phase). With no route, the next-phase consumer cannot find them; they accumulate. (4) Five issue types appearing in volume (`cited_by_drift` 11x, `frontmatter-schema-gap` 7x, `cross-file-consistency` 7x, `deploy-slow` 3x, `phase-died-mid-run` 6x) were on neither the auto-closeable whitelist nor the protected list, so the aging sweep never applied to them and they only ever escalated at 30 days, never closed at 14. Plus two naming-inconsistency entries (`orphan_links` vs `orphan_wiki_links`).

**State cleanup landed (`outputs/state_cleanup.py`).** Backed up `.vault-maintenance-state.json` to `.vault-maintenance-state.json.bak-2026-05-07`. Dropped the verified-confirmed entry (1). Dropped `gibbons-low-cited-by` (1). Renamed `orphan_links` to `orphan_wiki_links` on the two affected entries. Rewrote two `runbook` routes via the type-to-phase map. Backfilled `applies_to_phase` on 8 routeless entries. Result: 83 -> 81 pending issues. Counter `pending_issues_count` recomputed.

**Structural prevention (Tier-1: source-side authoring contract).** New cardinal rule under Step 3 of the scheduled-task prompt: every appended `pending_issues` entry MUST carry `id`, `type`, `opened_at`, and `applies_to_phase`. The `type` MUST be in the canonical type-to-phase map (defined in the new Verify housekeeping pre-pass); new types must extend the map in the same change that introduces them. The `applies_to_phase` MUST be in the eight-phase enum. Issues that should auto-close at 14 days but are not in the standard whitelist need `metadata.auto_close_eligible: true`. Issues that must never auto-close need `metadata.do_not_auto_close: true`. Phases that emit malformed entries (missing route, missing opened_at, invalid type, status: resolved left in state) take a `pending-issue-malformed` rubric hit recorded in CHANGELOG with phase name and issue id; the next Verify run normalizes them.

**Structural prevention (Tier-2: sink-side housekeeping pre-pass).** New Verify hard rule: a mandatory pending-issue housekeeping pre-pass runs BEFORE the aging sweep on every Verify tick. Four normalization rules in order: (1) drop entries with `status` in {`resolved`, `verified-confirmed`}; (2) normalize type aliases (`orphan_links` -> `orphan_wiki_links`, extensible); (3) backfill missing `applies_to_phase` from a fixed type-to-phase map covering all known types (with unmatched types defaulting to `verify` and emitting a `pending-issue-type-unknown` lesson candidate so the map gets extended); (4) reject invalid routes by rewriting via the same map, with provenance recorded in `_route_normalized_from`. Counters logged in CHANGELOG `{resolved_dropped, type_aliases_renamed, routes_backfilled, routes_rejected}` and BUILD_NARRATIVE.

**Structural prevention (Tier-3: expanded auto-closeable whitelist).** Added to Verify's auto-closeable set: `cited_by_drift`, `frontmatter-schema-gap`, `cross-file-consistency`, `deploy-slow`, `phase-died-mid-run`, `tooling-drift`, `verify-drift-check`, `midpage-not-indexed`, `verified-flip-anomaly`, `lecture-timeline-sweep-result`. Combined with the original five (`source-attribution-review-needed`, `low_cited_by`, `lesson-candidate`, `consolidation-review`, `persona-produced-nothing`) plus any issue carrying `metadata.auto_close_eligible: true`, the whitelist now covers every observed accumulating type. The protected list (never auto-close) is unchanged: `professor-feedback`, all `deploy-*` (except `deploy-slow` which is now auto-closeable), `cap-violation`, `personas-missing`, `missing-file-on-disk-source`, `metadata.do_not_auto_close: true`.

**Rubric tightening.** Verify's `pending_issue_aging` criterion (weight 0.10) now scores 5 only if BOTH the housekeeping pre-pass AND the aging sweep ran cleanly AND no entry older than 30 days remains AND no entry without `applies_to_phase` remains. Skipping either is `aging-sweep-skipped`. Leaving routeless entries is a new red flag `housekeeping-incomplete`. Step 2.2 also now requires recomputing `pending_issues_count` from the final array length on every run, with a state-corruption red flag if they drift.

**Forecast.** Of the 81 remaining issues, the aging sweep on the next Verify tick will likely auto-close around 25 (the cited_by_drift / phase-died-mid-run / frontmatter-schema-gap / cross-file-consistency / deploy-slow entries that are 14+ days old and were previously stuck because no whitelist entry covered them). Going forward, the source-side cardinal rule prevents new malformed entries from entering, the sink-side housekeeping fixes the legacy ones the rule cannot retroactively fix, and the expanded whitelist clears the type-class accumulation. Three layers of defense, each independent of the others.

**Files touched.** `.vault-maintenance-state.json` (script-driven cleanup, 83 -> 81 entries plus route backfills); scheduled-task prompt for `con-law-wiki-maintenance` (Verify hard-rules block expanded with housekeeping pre-pass; auto-closeable set expanded; cardinal rule added under Step 3; CHANGELOG and BUILD_NARRATIVE contracts extended with housekeeping counters); CHANGELOG.md (this entry); `.vault-maintenance-state.json.bak-2026-05-07` (backup written by cleanup script).

**Task status.** Paused before edits (so the in-flight Verify tick would not race the cleanup). Resume immediately after this entry per the user's earlier "resume the task" instruction model.

## Initial Setup -- 2026-04-16

**Action:** Full vault scaffolding and scheduled task creation

**1. Project Scoping**
- Single course (Professor Chandler, Con Law I, Spring 2026); auto-ingest mode; case briefs at 9-section case-briefer standard; 30-min runs.

**2. Source Material Inventory**
- Course export at `2026SP-LAW5488-10716---Constitutional-Law-2026-Apr-15_14-36-22-377/viewer/files/`. ~260+ files: 61 PPTXs, 66 PDFs at top level; ~130+ in `Uploaded Media/`; midterm rubric in `unfiled/`.

**3. Vault Structure Created**
- `Cases/`, `Topics/`, `Lectures/`, `Templates/`, `.obsidian/` (pre-existing).

**4. Templates Created**
- `Templates/Case Brief.md` (9-section), `Templates/Topic Page.md`, `Templates/Lecture Summary.md`.

**5. Three-File System Bootstrapped**
- `PROJECT_PRIMER.md`, `LESSONS.md` (12 lessons / 5 categories), `CHANGELOG.md`.

**6. State Files Created**
- `.vault-maintenance-state.json`, `.ingested-files.json`.

**7. Phase Rotation Designed**
- Ingest → Lint → Enrich → Expand → Synthesize → Verify.

**8. Scheduled Task Created**
- 30-min interval, tiered context loading; Ingest 3-5 files/run.

**Files created:** 8 | **Folders created:** 4

## Post-Setup Enhancements -- 2026-04-16

**1. Source Materials Reorganized**
- Moved to `Source Materials/` folder at vault root. 143 top-level + 165 in Uploaded Media. PROJECT_PRIMER and task prompt updated.

**2. Midpage MCP Integration**
- `analyzeOpinion` (mandatory Ingest, used Enrich/Verify), `findInOpinion` (keyword search). Key Quotations prefer Midpage-verified pin-cites.

**3. CourtListener MCP Integration**
- `find_cited_cases` / `find_citing_cases` (Expand), `search_case_law` (Synthesize).

**4. Adversarial Verify Protocol**
- Three personas (Staleness Auditor, Contradiction Hunter, Template Enforcer); each must return ≥1 finding.

**5. Run Scorecard System**
- `RUBRIC.md` with phase-specific criteria; `.run-scores.jsonl`; red flags trigger Lint pending issues; rolling 5-run median regression detection.

**6. Blog Documentation (BUILD_NARRATIVE.md)**
- 2-4 paragraph first-person past-tense narrative per run.

**Files created:** 3 (RUBRIC.md, BUILD_NARRATIVE.md, .run-scores.jsonl) | **Files updated:** 3 (PROJECT_PRIMER.md, CHANGELOG.md, scheduled task prompt)
